THE CITY · Qwen 27B, government, 300 days, game 1
A 10% tax on day 23 fills the store with 2,475 food that nobody ever spends. Everyone lives, everyone farms, and X4G is mayor from day 85 to the end.
24 founders · 300 days · with a town hall · seed 1 · qwen
